### 📖 Lesson 20: Double Object Pronouns & "Se La" Rule

**Subtitle**: Indirect Before Direct / Le->Se Rule  
**Professor's Note**: *"When combining both direct and indirect object pronouns in one sentence, Indirect ALWAYS comes first (People before Things)."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Order pronouns: Indirect before Direct
- Apply the "Se La" rule (change le/les to SE before lo/la)
- Place combined pronouns before conjugated verbs

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### The "Se La" Rule Explained

You cannot say "le lo" in Spanish because of awkward phonetic overlap. "Le" changes to "SE".

- Incorrect: Yo le lo doy. (I give it to him)
- Correct: Yo SE lo doy. (I give it to him)
- Formula: SE + lo / la / los / las

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **se lo** | `seh loh` | it to him/her/them | DOP+IOP combo |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `¿Le diste el libro a Juan? Sí, se lo di.`  
   **English**: Did you give the book to Juan? Yes, I gave it to him.  
   *Breakdown*: Le lo becomes se lo.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Ana**: "¿Me compras el café?" (*Will you buy me the coffee?*)
- **Beto**: "Sí, te lo compro ahora." (*Yes, I buy it for you now.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What does "le lo" change to?  
**Options**: me lo, te lo, se lo, nos lo  
**Correct Answer**: `se lo`  
**Explanation**: Le/les changes to SE before lo/la/los/las.

| 2 | **Tú me lo compras en el mercado cada sábado** | You buy it for me at the market every Saturday | `Tú (Subject) + me (Object) + lo (Object) + compras (Verb) + en (Place) + el (Place) + mercado (Place) + cada (Time) + sábado (Time)` | Shows standard first-person indirect object me preceding direct object lo. |
| 3 | **Ella te la vende en la tienda hoy mismo** | She sells it to you in the store today itself | `Ella (Subject) + te (Object) + la (Object) + vende (Verb) + en (Place) + la (Place) + tienda (Place) + hoy (Time) + mismo (Time)` | Combines second-person singular indirect object te with feminine direct object la. |
| 4 | **Nosotros nos los traemos a casa por la noche** | We bring them to us at home at night | `Nosotros (Subject) + nos (Object) + los (Object) + traemos (Verb) + a (Place) + casa (Place) + por (Time) + la (Time) + noche (Time)` | First-person plural reflexive/indirect object nos paired with masculine plural direct object los. |
| 5 | **Usted se las explica en clase por la mañana** | You explain them to her in class in the morning | `Usted (Subject) + se (Object) + las (Object) + explica (Verb) + en (Place) + clase (Place) + por (Time) + la (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Formal usted usage triggering the le->se transformation before feminine plural las. |

### 📖 Lesson 21: Reflexive Verbs & Daily Routine

**Subtitle**: Me, Te, Se, Nos, Os, Se + Routine Verbs  
**Professor's Note**: *"Reflexive verbs show that the subject performs and receives the action."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Learn reflexive pronouns (me, te, se, nos, os, se)
- Conjugate daily routine verbs (lavarse, levantarse, acostarse)
- Use definite articles with body parts (Me lavo las manos)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Reflexive Pronouns & Verb Matrix

| Subject | Reflexive Pronoun | Lavarse (to wash) | Levantarse (to get up) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | me | me lavo | me levanto |
| Tú | te | te lavas | te levantas |
| Él/Ella/Ud. | se | se lava | se levanta |
| Nosotros/as | nos | nos lavamos | nos levantamos |
| Ellos/as/Uds. | se | se lavan | se levantan |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **lavarse** | `lah-VAHR-seh` | to wash oneself | Reflexive verb |
| **levantarse** | `leh-vahn-TAHR-seh` | to get up | Reflexive verb |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Yo me levanto a las siete de la mañana.`  
   **English**: I get up at seven in the morning.  
   *Breakdown*: Reflexive me levanto.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Pedro**: "¿A qué hora te levantas?" (*What time do you get up?*)
- **Juan**: "Me levanto a las seis y me lavo la cara." (*I get up at six and wash my face.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What is the reflexive pronoun for "nosotros"?  
**Options**: me, te, se, nos  
**Correct Answer**: `nos`  
**Explanation**: Nosotros takes reflexive pronoun "nos".

# 📚 Part 6: Reflexive Verbs, Past Actions & Commands

> **Part 6 Overview**: Daily routines & reflexive pronouns, preterite vs imperfect past tenses, informal & formal imperative commands, and B1 bridge preparation.

---

### 📖 Lesson 22: Recent Past & Duration Formulas

**Subtitle**: Acabar de + Infinitive & Hace + Time + Que  
**Professor's Note**: *"Express recent past ("just done") and duration ("have been doing for X time") effortlessly without complex past tenses."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Use ACABAR DE + INFINITIVE for recent past
- Use HACE + time + QUE + Present for ongoing duration
- Form alternate duration sentences with desde hace

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Recent Past & Duration Rules

- Acabar de + Infinitive = To have just done something (Acabo de comer = I have just eaten).
- Hace + [time] + que + Present = Have been doing for [time] (Hace dos años que estudio español).
- Present + desde hace + [time] = Estudio español desde hace dos años.

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **acabo de** | `ah-KAH-boh deh` | I have just | Recent past auxiliary |
| **hace dos años** | `AH-seh dohs AH-nyohs` | for two years | Duration phrase |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Acabamos de llegar a la universidad.`  
   **English**: We have just arrived at university.  
   *Breakdown*: Acabar (nosotros) + de + llegar.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Luis**: "¿Tienes hambre?" (*Are you hungry?*)
- **Ana**: "No, acabo de comer una manzana." (*No, I have just eaten an apple.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: Translate "I have just eaten":  
**Options**: Acabo de comer, Como reciente, Hago comer, Fui a comer  
**Correct Answer**: `Acabo de comer`  
**Explanation**: Acabar de + infinitive expresses recent past.

| 24 | **Llevas tres meses entrenando para un maratón largo en el parque ayer** | You have been training for a long marathon in the park yesterday for three months | `Llevas (Verb) + tres (Time) + meses (Time) + entrenando (Verb) + para (Other) + un (Object) + maratón (Object) + largo (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + parque (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Second-person singular duration featuring athletic context. |
| 25 | **Lleva cinco años coleccionando sellos raros en su estudio anoche** | He has been collecting rare stamps in his studio last night for five years | `Lleva (Verb) + cinco (Time) + años (Time) + coleccionando (Verb) + sellos (Object) + raros (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + estudio (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Third-person singular duration statement focusing on hobbies. |
| 26 | **Tú acabas de perder las llaves doradas en el parque anoche** | You just lost the golden keys in the park last night | `Tú (Subject) + acabas (Verb) + de (Verb) + perder (Verb) + las (Object) + llaves (Object) + doradas (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + parque (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Uses 'acabas de' with a stem-changing verb (perder) and feminine plural object. |
| 27 | **Ella lleva tres horas estudiando la gramática avanzada en su casa ahora** | She has been studying advanced grammar at her house for three hours now | `Ella (Subject) + lleva (Verb) + tres (Time) + horas (Time) + estudiando (Verb) + la (Object) + gramática (Object) + avanzada (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + casa (Place) + ahora (Time)` | Illustrates 'llevar + duration + gerund' expressing an ongoing action over a period of time. |
| 28 | **Nosotros llevamos dos semanas preparando el proyecto grande en la escuela siempre** | We have been preparing the big project at school for two weeks always | `Nosotros (Subject) + llevamos (Verb) + dos (Time) + semanas (Time) + preparando (Verb) + el (Object) + proyecto (Object) + grande (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + escuela (Place) + siempre (Time)` | First-person plural application of 'llevar + duration + gerund'. |
| 29 | **Carlos acaba de comprar un coche nuevo en el concesionario esta mañana** | Carlos just bought a new car at the dealership this morning | `Carlos (Subject) + acaba (Verb) + de (Verb) + comprar (Verb) + un (Object) + coche (Object) + nuevo (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + concesionario (Place) + esta (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Uses a third-person proper noun subject with 'acabar de' and a specific time expression. |
| 30 | **Hace cinco días que esperamos la carta oficial en el buzón pacientemente** | It has been five days that we wait for the official letter in the mailbox patiently | `Hace (Verb) + cinco (Time) + días (Time) + que (Other) + esperamos (Verb) + la (Object) + carta (Object) + oficial (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + buzón (Place) + pacientemente (Time)` | Demonstrates the 'hace + time + que + verb' structure for ongoing actions. |
| 31 | **Hace un año que vivo en esta ciudad hermosa tranquilamente** | It has been a year that I live in this beautiful city quietly | `Hace (Verb) + un (Time) + año (Time) + que (Other) + vivo (Verb) + en (Place) + esta (Place) + ciudad (Place) + hermosa (Place) + tranquilamente (Time)` | Expresses duration of residence using 'hace + time + que'. |
| 32 | **Yo acabo de terminar la tarea difícil en mi cuarto hoy** | I just finished the difficult homework in my bedroom today | `Yo (Subject) + acabo (Verb) + de (Verb) + terminar (Verb) + la (Object) + tarea (Object) + difícil (Object) + en (Place) + mi (Place) + cuarto (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Standard B2 sentence combining 'acabar de', modified direct object, and place. |
| 33 | **Tú llevas cuatro meses buscando un trabajo mejor en Madrid ahora** | You have been looking for a better job in Madrid for four months now | `Tú (Subject) + llevas (Verb) + cuatro (Time) + meses (Time) + buscando (Verb) + un (Object) + trabajo (Object) + mejor (Object) + en (Place) + Madrid (Place) + ahora (Time)` | Combines 'llevar + duration + gerund' with a comparative adjective object. |
| 34 | **María acaba de recibir un paquete pesado en el portal ayer** | Maria just received a heavy package at the doorway yesterday | `María (Subject) + acaba (Verb) + de (Verb) + recibir (Verb) + un (Object) + paquete (Object) + pesado (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + portal (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Simple past action using 'acabar de' with a proper noun subject. |
| 35 | **Hace tres horas que estudiamos la lección compleja en la biblioteca siempre** | It has been three hours that we study the complex lesson in the library always | `Hace (Verb) + tres (Time) + horas (Time) + que (Other) + estudiamos (Verb) + la (Object) + lección (Object) + compleja (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + biblioteca (Place) + siempre (Time)` | Demonstrates duration with 'hace... que' paired with an 'nosotros' implied verb. |
| 36 | **Nosotros acabamos de limpiar la cocina entera por la mañana** | We just cleaned the entire kitchen in the morning | `Nosotros (Subject) + acabamos (Verb) + de (Verb) + limpiar (Verb) + la (Object) + cocina (Object) + entera (Object) + por (Time) + la (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Uses 'acabamos de' with a direct object modified by an adjective. |
| 37 | **Ella lleva dos años enseñando el idioma español en Valencia hoy** | She has been teaching the Spanish language in Valencia today | `Ella (Subject) + lleva (Verb) + dos (Time) + años (Time) + enseñando (Verb) + el (Object) + idioma (Object) + español (Object) + en (Place) + Valencia (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Shows 'llevar + duration + gerund' with a proper location and time marker. |
| 38 | **Tú acabas de escribir un ensayo largo en la clase anoche** | You just wrote a long essay in class last night | `Tú (Subject) + acabas (Verb) + de (Verb) + escribir (Verb) + un (Object) + ensayo (Object) + largo (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + clase (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Practices second-person singular 'acabas de' with a descriptive object noun phrase. |
| 39 | **Hace diez minutos que salimos del restaurante elegante ahora** | It has been ten minutes that we left the elegant restaurant now | `Hace (Verb) + diez (Time) + minutos (Time) + que (Other) + salimos (Verb) + del (Place) + restaurante (Place) + elegante (Place) + ahora (Time)` | Short duration expressed with 'hace + time + que' combined with a place preposition contraction (del). |
| 40 | **Yo llevo seis meses leyendo una novela histórica en casa siempre** | I have been reading a historical novel at home for six months always | `Yo (Subject) + llevo (Verb) + seis (Time) + meses (Time) + leyendo (Verb) + una (Object) + novela (Object) + histórica (Object) + en (Place) + casa (Place) + siempre (Time)` | First-person application of 'llevar + duration + gerund' with a descriptive book title object. |

### 📖 Lesson 23: Present Duration & Time Queries

**Subtitle**: ¿Desde cuándo...? & Hay Que + Infinitive  
**Professor's Note**: *"Hay que expresses general impersonal necessity ("one must / it is necessary to")."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Query duration with ¿Desde cuándo...?
- Use impersonal obligation: HAY QUE + INFINITIVE
- Contrast personal (tener que) vs impersonal (hay que)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Hay Que vs Tener Que

Tener que is personal (Tengo que estudiar = I must study). Hay que is universal (Hay que estudiar = One must study).

- Hay que practicar todos los días. (It is necessary to practice every day).
- ¿Desde cuándo estudias español? (Since when have you been studying Spanish?)

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **hay que** | `ey keh` | one must / it is necessary to | Impersonal obligation |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Hay que hablar en español en la clase.`  
   **English**: One must speak in Spanish in class.  
   *Breakdown*: Impersonal rule.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Profesor**: "Para aprender, hay que estudiar mucho." (*To learn, one must study a lot.*)
- **Alumno**: "Sí, profesor, tengo que practicar." (*Yes, professor, I have to practice.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: Which phrase expresses general impersonal necessity?  
**Options**: Tengo que, Hay que, Debo que, Quiero que  
**Correct Answer**: `Hay que`  
**Explanation**: Hay que + infinitive is impersonal.

### 📖 Lesson 24: Formal Commands & Unequal Comparisons

**Subtitle**: Usted Mandatos & Más... Que Expressions  
**Professor's Note**: *"Formal commands swap vowel endings: -AR verbs take -e/-en, -ER/-IR verbs take -a/-an."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Form formal Usted/Ustedes commands
- Apply vowel swap rules (-AR -> -e, -ER/-IR -> -a)
- Construct unequal comparisons (más... que / menos... que)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Formal Command Vowel Swap Table

| Verb Type | Infinitive | Usted Command (Singular) | Ustedes Command (Plural) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| -AR Verbs | hablar | ¡Hable! | ¡Hablen! |
| -ER Verbs | comer | ¡Coma! | ¡Coman! |
| -IR Verbs | escribir | ¡Escriba! | ¡Escriban! |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **¡Hable!** | `AH-bleh` | Speak! (formal) | Usted command |
| **más... que** | `mahs keh` | more... than | Comparison |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `¡Coma la sopa, por favor!`  
   **English**: Eat the soup, please (formal)!  
   *Breakdown*: Formal command from comer.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Doctor**: "¡Beba más agua y descanse!" (*Drink more water and rest (formal)!*)
- **Paciente**: "Sí doctor, voy a hacerlo." (*Yes doctor, I will do it.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: Form formal Usted command for "hablar":  
**Options**: ¡Habla!, ¡Hable!, ¡Hablo!, ¡Hablen!  
**Correct Answer**: `¡Hable!`  
**Explanation**: -AR verbs change to -e for formal singular command.

### 📖 Lesson 25: Informal Tú Commands (Mandatos)

**Subtitle**: 8 Irregular Tú Commands: Haz, Ten, Pon, Sal, Ve, Di, Ven, Sé  
**Professor's Note**: *"Affirmative informal commands use the 3rd person singular present form (Habla, Come). Memorize the 8 irregular command words!"*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Form affirmative informal tú commands
- Memorize the 8 irregular command short forms
- Form negative tú commands with no + sub

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### The 8 Irregular Affirmative Tú Commands

| Infinitive | Irregular Tú Command | English Command |
| --- | --- | --- |
| hacer | Haz | Do! / Make! |
| tener | Ten | Have! / Hold! |
| poner | Pon | Put! / Set! |
| salir | Sal | Exit! / Leave! |
| ir | Ve | Go! |
| decir | Di | Say! / Tell! |
| venir | Ven | Come! |
| ser | Sé | Be! |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **¡Haz!** | `ahs` | Do! / Make! | Irregular command |
| **¡Pon!** | `pohn` | Put! / Set! | Irregular command |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `¡Haz la tarea y pon los libros aquí!`  
   **English**: Do the homework and put the books here!  
   *Breakdown*: Two irregular commands.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Madre**: "¡Haz tu cama y sal a jugar!" (*Make your bed and go out to play!*)
- **Hijo**: "¡Ya voy, mamá!" (*I'm coming, mom!*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What is the irregular tú command for "hacer"?  
**Options**: hace, haz, haga, hazas  
**Correct Answer**: `haz`  
**Explanation**: Hacer irregular command is Haz.

| 32 | **Sé amable con todos en la fiesta** | Be kind to everyone at the party | `Sé (Verb) + amable (Object) + con (Object) + todos (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + fiesta (Place)` | Highlights the irregular affirmative command 'sé' (from ser) with a descriptive adjective complement. |
| 33 | **Ve al supermercado local esta mañana** | Go to the local supermarket this morning | `Ve (Verb) + al (Place) + supermercado (Place) + local (Place) + esta (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Applies the irregular affirmative command 've' (from ir) with a modified place phrase. |
| 34 | **Habla español fluido con los turistas ahora** | Speak fluent Spanish with the tourists now | `Habla (Verb) + español (Object) + fluido (Object) + con (Place) + los (Place) + turistas (Place) + ahora (Time)` | Demonstrates a regular -ar affirmative tú command with a descriptive object and context. |
| 35 | **Come una sopa caliente en la cocina** | Eat a hot soup in the kitchen | `Come (Verb) + una (Object) + sopa (Object) + caliente (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + cocina (Place)` | Uses a regular -er affirmative tú command paired with a specific food item and location. |
| 36 | **Escribe una carta larga a tus padres** | Write a long letter to your parents | `Escribe (Verb) + una (Object) + carta (Object) + larga (Object) + a (Object) + tus (Object) + padres (Object)` | Features a regular -ir affirmative tú command targeting an indirect recipient object. |
| 37 | **Compra frutas frescas en el mercado hoy** | Buy fresh fruits at the market today | `Compra (Verb) + frutas (Object) + frescas (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + mercado (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Reinforces standard affirmative commands with plural direct objects and time markers. |
| 38 | **Lee este artículo interesante en tu oficina** | Read this interesting article in your office | `Lee (Verb) + este (Object) + artículo (Object) + interesante (Object) + en (Place) + tu (Place) + oficina (Place)` | Practices regular -er verbs in the imperative mood with demonstrative adjectives. |
| 39 | **Abre la ventana grande por la mañana** | Open the large window in the morning | `Abre (Verb) + la (Object) + ventana (Object) + grande (Object) + por (Time) + la (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Combines a regular -ir imperative verb with a descriptive object and routine time phrase. |
| 40 | **Guarda los documentos importantes en el cajón** | Keep the important documents in the drawer | `Guarda (Verb) + los (Object) + documentos (Object) + importantes (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + cajón (Place)` | Uses an affirmative command with a plural direct object modified by an adjective. |

### 📖 Lesson 26: Preterite Past Tense Regulars

**Subtitle**: -é, -aste, -ó, -amos, -aron & -í, -iste, -ió, -imos, -ieron  
**Professor's Note**: *"The preterite tense narrates completed past actions that occurred at a specific point in time."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Master regular -AR preterite endings (-é, -aste, -ó, -amos, -aron)
- Master regular -ER/-IR preterite endings (-í, -iste, -ió, -imos, -ieron)
- Identify past time markers (ayer, anoche, la semana pasada)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Preterite Regular Endings Matrix

| Subject | -AR Preterite (Hablar) | -ER/-IR Preterite (Comer / Vivir) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | hablé (I spoke) | comí / viví (I ate / lived) |
| Tú | hablaste | comiste / viviste |
| Él/Ella/Ud. | habló | comió / vivió |
| Nosotros/as | hablamos | comimos / vivimos |
| Ellos/as/Uds. | hablaron | comieron / vivieron |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **ayer** | `ah-YEHR` | yesterday | Preterite time marker |
| **anoche** | `ah-NOH-cheh` | last night | Preterite time marker |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Ayer hablé con el profesor y comí tacos.`  
   **English**: Yesterday I spoke with the professor and ate tacos.  
   *Breakdown*: Preterite past actions.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Juan**: "¿Qué hiciste ayer? ¿Hablaste con Ana?" (*What did you do yesterday? Did you speak with Ana?*)
- **Pedro**: "Sí, hablé con ella anoche." (*Yes, I spoke with her last night.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What is the "Yo" preterite form of "hablar"?  
**Options**: habló, hablé, hablaste, hablo  
**Correct Answer**: `hablé`  
**Explanation**: Regular -AR verbs take "-é" for Yo preterite.

#### 🧩 Interactive Sentence Builder Database (Lesson 26: 40 Exercises)

| # | Spanish Sentence | English Translation | Token Breakdown | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **Yo estudié matemáticas difíciles en la biblioteca ayer** | I studied difficult mathematics in the library yesterday | `Yo (Subject) + estudié (Verb) + matemáticas (Object) + difíciles (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + biblioteca (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Regular -ar preterite verb with a plural adjective modifier on the object. |
| 2 | **Tú comiste una paella deliciosa en el restaurante anoche** | You ate a delicious paella at the restaurant last night | `Tú (Subject) + comiste (Verb) + una (Object) + paella (Object) + deliciosa (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + restaurante (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Regular -er preterite ending for tu (-iste) with a specific time marker. |
| 3 | **Ella escribió un ensayo largo en su oficina hoy** | She wrote a long essay in her office today | `Ella (Subject) + escribió (Verb) + un (Object) + ensayo (Object) + largo (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + oficina (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Regular -ir preterite third-person singular ending (-ió). |
| 4 | **Nosotros compramos unos zapatos nuevos en el centro anteayer** | We bought some new shoes downtown the day before yesterday | `Nosotros (Subject) + compramos (Verb) + unos (Object) + zapatos (Object) + nuevos (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + centro (Place) + anteayer (Time)` | Regular -ar first-person plural preterite ending (-amos), identical to present. |
| 5 | **Ellos vendieron su coche antiguo en la feria pasada** | They sold their old car at the last fair | `Ellos (Subject) + vendieron (Verb) + su (Object) + coche (Object) + antiguo (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + feria (Place) + pasada (Place)` | Regular -er preterite third-person plural ending (-ieron). |
| 6 | **Usted recibió una carta importante en su casa temprano** | You received an important letter at your house early | `Usted (Subject) + recibió (Verb) + una (Object) + carta (Object) + importante (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + casa (Place) + temprano (Time)` | Regular -ir preterite form for formal you (usted). |
| 7 | **Yo viajé a Madrid durante las vacaciones pasadas** | I traveled to Madrid during the past vacations | `Yo (Subject) + viajé (Verb) + a (Place) + Madrid (Place) + durante (Time) + las (Time) + vacaciones (Time) + pasadas (Time)` | Preterite regular -ar verb with graphic accent on the first person singular (-é). |
| 8 | **Tú leíste un libro interesante en el parque ayer** | You read an interesting book in the park yesterday | `Tú (Subject) + leíste (Verb) + un (Object) + libro (Object) + interesante (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + parque (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Leer takes an orthographic change in the third person, but second person singular (-iste) is standard. |
| 9 | **El profesor explicó una lección compleja en el aula hoy** | The professor explained a complex lesson in the classroom today | `El (Subject) + profesor (Subject) + explicó (Verb) + una (Object) + lección (Object) + compleja (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + aula (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Noun phrase subject with a regular -ar preterite verb. |
| 10 | **Nosotros decidimos una estrategia nueva en la reunión anoche** | We decided on a new strategy in the meeting last night | `Nosotros (Subject) + decidimos (Verb) + una (Object) + estrategia (Object) + nueva (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + reunión (Place) + anoche (Time)` | First-person plural -ir preterite form (-imos) looks identical to present tense. |
| 11 | **Ella preparó una sopa caliente en su cocina esta mañana** | She prepared a hot soup in her kitchen this morning | `Ella (Subject) + preparó (Verb) + una (Object) + sopa (Object) + caliente (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + cocina (Place) + esta (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Regular -ar verb ending with stress on the final syllable (-ó). |
| 12 | **Tú escuchaste una canción hermosa en la radio anoche** | You listened to a beautiful song on the radio last night | `Tú (Subject) + escuchaste (Verb) + una (Object) + canción (Object) + hermosa (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + radio (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Regular -ar ending for second person singular (-aste). |
| 13 | **Ellos rompieron una ventana grande en el patio ayer** | They broke a large window in the patio yesterday | `Ellos (Subject) + rompieron (Verb) + una (Object) + ventana (Object) + grande (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + patio (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Regular -er preterite plural ending (-ieron). |
| 14 | **Yo alquilé un apartamento luminoso en la costa el año pasado** | I rented a bright apartment on the coast last year | `Yo (Subject) + alquilé (Verb) + un (Object) + apartamento (Object) + luminoso (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + costa (Place) + el (Time) + año (Time) + pasado (Time)` | Extended time phrase functioning as a single temporal unit. |
| 15 | **Ella abrió una tienda nueva en el centro comercial hoy** | She opened a new store in the mall today | `Ella (Subject) + abrió (Verb) + una (Object) + tienda (Object) + nueva (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + centro (Place) + comercial (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Regular -ir preterite third-person singular (-ió). |
| 16 | **Nosotros cantamos una canción popular en el concierto anoche** | We sang a popular song at the concert last night | `Nosotros (Subject) + cantamos (Verb) + una (Object) + canción (Object) + popular (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + concierto (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Regular -ar verb in first-person plural. |
| 17 | **Tú bebiste un jugo natural en la cafetería por la tarde** | You drank a natural juice at the cafeteria in the afternoon | `Tú (Subject) + bebiste (Verb) + un (Object) + jugo (Object) + natural (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + cafetería (Place) + por (Time) + la (Time) + tarde (Time)` | Regular -er verb in second-person singular preterite. |
| 18 | **Ellos firmaron un contrato importante en la oficina ayer** | They signed an important contract in the office yesterday | `Ellos (Subject) + firmaron (Verb) + un (Object) + contrato (Object) + importante (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + oficina (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Regular -ar third-person plural preterite (-aron). |
| 19 | **Yo sufrí un tráfico pesado en la autopista esta mañana** | I suffered heavy traffic on the highway this morning | `Yo (Subject) + sufrí (Verb) + un (Object) + tráfico (Object) + pesado (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + autopista (Place) + esta (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Regular -ir first-person singular preterite (-í). |
| 20 | **Ella pintó una pared blanca en su dormitorio la semana pasada** | She painted a white wall in her bedroom last week | `Ella (Subject) + pintó (Verb) + una (Object) + pared (Object) + blanca (Object) + en (Place) + su (Place) + dormitorio (Place) + la (Time) + semana (Time) + pasada (Time)` | Regular -ar verb showing completed past action with a specific time frame. |

| 22 | **Tú organizaste una fiesta sorpresa en tu casa el sábado** | You organized a surprise party at your house on Saturday | `Tú (Subject) + organizaste (Verb) + una (Object) + fiesta (Object) + sorpresa (Object) + en (Place) + tu (Place) + casa (Place) + el (Time) + sábado (Time)` | Regular -ar preterite second-person singular with a compound noun object. |
| 23 | **Ellos compartieron una pizza grande en el restaurante anoche** | They shared a large pizza at the restaurant last night | `Ellos (Subject) + compartieron (Verb) + una (Object) + pizza (Object) + grande (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + restaurante (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Regular -ir third-person plural preterite ending. |
| 24 | **Yo saludé a un viejo amigo en la calle ayer** | I greeted an old friend on the street yesterday | `Yo (Subject) + saludé (Verb) + a (Object) + un (Object) + viejo (Object) + amigo (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + calle (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Demonstrates personal 'a' preceding a direct object person. |
| 25 | **Ella discutió un problema complejo en la reunión hoy** | She discussed a complex problem in the meeting today | `Ella (Subject) + discutió (Verb) + un (Object) + problema (Object) + complejo (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + reunión (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Regular -ir preterite verb with masculine noun ending in -ma. |
| 26 | **Yo compré un libro interesante en la librería ayer** | I bought an interesting book at the bookstore yesterday | `Yo (Subject) + compré (Verb) + un (Object) + libro (Object) + interesante (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + librería (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Demonstrates the regular -ar preterite ending for 'yo' (-é) with a descriptive object and past time marker. |
| 27 | **Tú estudiaste la lección difícil en tu habitación anoche** | You studied the difficult lesson in your room last night | `Tú (Subject) + estudiaste (Verb) + la (Object) + lección (Object) + difícil (Object) + en (Place) + tu (Place) + habitación (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Practices the regular -ar preterite ending for 'tú' (-aste) combined with a location and time phrase. |
| 28 | **Ella preparó una cena deliciosa en la cocina temprano** | She prepared a delicious dinner in the kitchen early | `Ella (Subject) + preparó (Verb) + una (Object) + cena (Object) + deliciosa (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + cocina (Place) + temprano (Time)` | Highlights the regular -ar preterite third-person singular ending (-ó) with a written accent. |
| 29 | **Nosotros escuchamos música clásica en el salón hoy** | We listened to classical music in the living room today | `Nosotros (Subject) + escuchamos (Verb) + música (Object) + clásica (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + salón (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Shows the first-person plural form (-amos) for -ar verbs in the preterite, which is identical to the present tense. |
| 30 | **Ellos visitaron un museo famoso en Madrid recientemente** | They visited a famous museum in Madrid recently | `Ellos (Subject) + visitaron (Verb) + un (Object) + museo (Object) + famoso (Object) + en (Place) + Madrid (Place) + recientemente (Time)` | Practices the regular third-person plural preterite ending (-aron) for -ar verbs. |
| 31 | **Yo bebí un jugo fresco en la terraza por la mañana** | I drank a fresh juice on the terrace in the morning | `Yo (Subject) + bebí (Verb) + un (Object) + jugo (Object) + fresco (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + terraza (Place) + por (Time) + la (Time) + mañana (Time)` | Introduces the regular -er preterite first-person singular ending (-í with an accent). |
| 32 | **Tú comiste una paella excelente en el restaurante esta tarde** | You ate an excellent paella at the restaurant this afternoon | `Tú (Subject) + comiste (Verb) + una (Object) + paella (Object) + excelente (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + restaurante (Place) + esta (Time) + tarde (Time)` | Demonstrates the regular -er preterite second-person singular ending (-iste). |
| 33 | **Usted vendió su coche antiguo en el mercado el mes pasado** | You sold your old car at the market last month | `Usted (Subject) + vendió (Verb) + su (Object) + coche (Object) + antiguo (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + mercado (Place) + el (Time) + mes (Time) + pasado (Time)` | Shows the formal singular preterite third-person ending (-ió) for an -er verb. |
| 34 | **Nosotros aprendimos una regla nueva en la clase anteayer** | We learned a new rule in class the day before yesterday | `Nosotros (Subject) + aprendimos (Verb) + una (Object) + regla (Object) + nueva (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + clase (Place) + anteayer (Time)` | Highlights the first-person plural preterite ending (-imos) for -er/-ir verbs. |
| 35 | **Ellas corrieron un maratón largo por la ciudad el domingo** | They ran a long marathon through the city on Sunday | `Ellas (Subject) + corrieron (Verb) + un (Object) + maratón (Object) + largo (Object) + por (Place) + la (Place) + ciudad (Place) + el (Time) + domingo (Time)` | Demonstrates the regular third-person plural preterite ending (-ieron) for -er verbs. |
| 36 | **Yo viví tres años maravillosos en Barcelona durante mi juventud** | I lived three wonderful years in Barcelona during my youth | `Yo (Subject) + viví (Verb) + tres (Object) + años (Object) + maravillosos (Object) + en (Place) + Barcelona (Place) + durante (Time) + mi (Time) + juventud (Time)` | Introduces the regular -ir preterite first-person singular ending (-í). |
| 37 | **Tú escribiste una carta larga a tu madre el sábado** | You wrote a long letter to your mother on Saturday | `Tú (Subject) + escribiste (Verb) + una (Object) + carta (Object) + larga (Object) + a (Other) + tu (Other) + madre (Other) + el (Time) + sábado (Time)` | Practices the regular -ir preterite second-person singular ending (-iste). |
| 38 | **El profesor explicó la gramática compleja en el aula hoy** | The teacher explained the complex grammar in the classroom today | `El (Subject) + profesor (Subject) + explicó (Verb) + la (Object) + gramática (Object) + compleja (Object) + en (Place) + el (Place) + aula (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Uses a noun phrase subject with a regular -ar verb in the preterite. |
| 39 | **Nosotros decidimos un plan perfecto para el viaje entonces** | We decided on a perfect plan for the trip back then | `Nosotros (Subject) + decidimos (Verb) + un (Object) + plan (Object) + perfecto (Object) + para (Other) + el (Other) + viaje (Other) + entonces (Time)` | Shows the first-person plural preterite ending (-imos) for the -ir verb 'decidir'. |
| 40 | **Los niños recibieron muchos regalos sorpresa en la fiesta anoche** | The children received many surprise gifts at the party last night | `Los (Subject) + niños (Subject) + recibieron (Verb) + muchos (Object) + regalos (Object) + sorpresa (Object) + en (Place) + la (Place) + fiesta (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Demonstrates the regular third-person plural preterite ending (-ieron) for -ir verbs with a plural noun subject. |

### 📖 Lesson 27: Present Perfect Tense (El Pretérito Perfecto Compuesto)

**Subtitle**: Recent Actions, Life Experiences, and Auxiliary HABER  
**Professor's Note**: *"The Present Perfect bridges past actions with present relevance! Think of it as 'have done' in English."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Master conjugation of auxiliary verb *haber* (he, has, ha, hemos, han)
- Form regular past participles (-ar -> -ado, -er/-ir -> -ido)
- Memorize 10 key irregular past participles (*abierto, dicho, escrito, hecho, visto, vuelto, etc.*)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Auxiliary HABER & Past Participle Formation

The Present Perfect is formed by combining the present tense of **haber** with the past participle. Unlike English, **never** insert any words (pronouns or adverbs) between *haber* and the participle!

| Pronoun | Haber Conjugation | Regular -AR Participle | Regular -ER/-IR Participle | Full Sentence Example |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | **he** | hablado | comido | Yo he hablado con el profesor |
| Tú | **has** | trabajado | vivido | Tú has trabajado mucho |
| Él / Ella / Ud. | **ha** | viajado | aprendido | Ella ha aprendido español |
| Nosotros | **hemos** | cenado | asistido | Nosotros hemos cenado temprano |
| Ellos / Ellas / Uds. | **han** | ganado | salido | Ellos han salido a la calle |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Word Order with Pronouns**: Reflexive and object pronouns ALWAYS go before *haber*!  
> *Example*: **Me he levantado** a las siete. (NOT: *He me levantado*)

##### Irregular Past Participles Table

| Verb | Infinitive | Irregular Participle | Example Sentence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Abrir | to open | **abierto** | He abierto la ventana. |
| Decir | to say/tell | **dicho** | Ella ha dicho la verdad. |
| Escribir | to write | **escrito** | Hemos escrito un informe. |
| Hacer | to do/make | **hecho** | ¿Qué has hecho hoy? |
| Morir | to die | **muerto** | El gato ha muerto. |
| Poneros | to put/place | **puesto** | He puesto las llaves aquí. |
| Romper | to break | **roto** | Juan ha roto el vaso. |
| Ver | to see/watch | **visto** | Hemos visto esa película. |
| Volver | to return | **vuelto** | María ha vuelto de España. |
| Resolver | to resolve | **resuelto** | Han resuelto el problema. |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **hecho** | `EH-choh` | done / made | Irregular participle of *hacer* |
| **dicho** | `DEE-choh` | said / told | Irregular participle of *decir* |
| **escrito** | `ehs-KREE-toh` | written | Irregular participle of *escribir* |
| **visto** | `BEES-toh` | seen / watched | Irregular participle of *ver* |
| **abierto** | `ah-BYEHR-toh` | opened / open | Irregular participle of *abrir* |
| **puesto** | `PWEHS-toh` | put / placed | Irregular participle of *poner* |
| **vuelto** | `BWEHL-toh` | returned | Irregular participle of *volver* |
| **todavía no** | `toh-dah-BEE-ah noh` | not yet | Common present perfect trigger |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Yo he preparado la cena en la cocina esta tarde.`  
   **English**: I have prepared dinner in the kitchen this afternoon.  
   *Breakdown*: Recent action using present perfect.

2. **Spanish**: `Ella ha escrito tres cartas importantes hoy.`  
   **English**: She has written three important letters today.  
   *Breakdown*: Irregular participle *escrito*.

3. **Spanish**: `Nosotros hemos visto un gato blanco en el jardín.`  
   **English**: We have seen a white cat in the garden.  
   *Breakdown*: Irregular participle *visto*.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Carlos**: "¿Has probado la paella valenciana alguna vez?" (*Have you ever tried Valencian paella?*)
- **Ana**: "Sí, he comido paella deliciosa en Madrid este verano." (*Yes, I have eaten delicious paella in Madrid this summer.*)
- **Carlos**: "¡Qué bueno! Yo todavía no la he probado." (*How wonderful! I haven't tried it yet.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: Which is the correct sentence for "We have written the report"?  
**Options**: Hemos escribido el informe, Hemos escrito el informe, He escrito el informe, Hemos escribiendo el informe  
**Correct Answer**: `Hemos escrito el informe`  
**Explanation**: Escribir has the irregular past participle *escrito*, paired with *hemos* for nosotros.

| # | Spanish Sentence | English Translation | Token Breakdown | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **Yo he comido una manzana en la cocina hoy** | I have eaten an apple in the kitchen today | `Yo (Subject) + he comido (Verb) + una manzana (Object) + en la cocina (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Present perfect regular -er participle. |
| 2 | **Tú has estudiado español en la biblioteca esta mañana** | You have studied Spanish in the library this morning | `Tú (Subject) + has estudiado (Verb) + español (Object) + en la biblioteca (Place) + esta mañana (Time)` | Present perfect regular -ar participle. |
| 3 | **Él ha comprado un coche en el concesionario este mes** | He has bought a car at the dealership this month | `Él (Subject) + ha comprado (Verb) + un coche (Object) + en el concesionario (Place) + este mes (Time)` | Third person singular present perfect. |
| 4 | **Ella ha escrito una carta en su cuarto esta tarde** | She has written a letter in her room this afternoon | `Ella (Subject) + ha escrito (Verb) + una carta (Object) + en su cuarto (Place) + esta tarde (Time)` | Irregular participle escrito. |
| 5 | **Usted ha abierto la puerta en la entrada ahora mismo** | You have opened the door at the entrance right now | `Usted (Subject) + ha abierto (Verb) + la puerta (Object) + en la entrada (Place) + ahora mismo (Time)` | Formal usted with irregular abierto. |
| 6 | **Nosotros hemos visto un eclipse en el parque anoche** | We have seen an eclipse in the park last night | `Nosotros (Subject) + hemos visto (Verb) + un eclipse (Object) + en el parque (Place) + anoche (Time)` | Irregular participle visto. |
| 7 | **Ellos han hecho la tarea en la mesa esta noche** | They have done the homework at the table tonight | `Ellos (Subject) + han hecho (Verb) + la tarea (Object) + en la mesa (Place) + esta noche (Time)` | Irregular participle hecho. |
| 8 | **Yo he puesto las llaves en la mesa hoy** | I have placed the keys on the table today | `Yo (Subject) + he puesto (Verb) + las llaves (Object) + en la mesa (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Irregular participle puesto. |
| 9 | **Tú has vuelto de Madrid en avión esta semana** | You have returned from Madrid by plane this week | `Tú (Subject) + has vuelto (Verb) + de Madrid (Object) + en avión (Place) + esta semana (Time)` | Irregular participle vuelto. |
| 10 | **Él ha dicho la verdad en el juicio ayer** | He has told the truth in court yesterday | `Él (Subject) + ha dicho (Verb) + la verdad (Object) + en el juicio (Place) + ayer (Time)` | Irregular participle dicho. |
| 11 | **Ella ha roto el vaso en el comedor hoy** | She has broken the glass in the dining room today | `Ella (Subject) + ha roto (Verb) + el vaso (Object) + en el comedor (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Irregular participle roto. |
| 12 | **Nosotros hemos recibido un paquete en la oficina esta mañana** | We have received a package at the office this morning | `Nosotros (Subject) + hemos recibido (Verb) + un paquete (Object) + en la oficina (Place) + esta mañana (Time)` | Regular -ir participle recibido. |
| 13 | **Ellos han vivido una buena vida en España durante cinco años** | They have lived a good life in Spain for five years | `Ellos (Subject) + han vivido (Verb) + una buena vida (Object) + en España (Place) + durante cinco años (Time)` | Duration trigger with present perfect. |
| 14 | **Yo he abierto la ventana en el salón ahora** | I have opened the window in the living room now | `Yo (Subject) + he abierto (Verb) + la ventana (Object) + en el salón (Place) + ahora (Time)` | Irregular abierto. |
| 15 | **Tú has preparado el café en la cocina temprano** | You have prepared coffee in the kitchen early | `Tú (Subject) + has preparado (Verb) + el café (Object) + en la cocina (Place) + temprano (Time)` | Regular -ar participle preparado. |

| 17 | **Ella ha vendido su coche en la ciudad esta tarde** | She has sold her car in the city this afternoon | `Ella (Subject) + ha vendido (Verb) + su coche (Object) + en la ciudad (Place) + esta tarde (Time)` | Regular -er participle vendido. |
| 18 | **Nosotros hemos organizado una fiesta en el jardín hoy** | We have organized a party in the garden today | `Nosotros (Subject) + hemos organizado (Verb) + una fiesta (Object) + en el jardín (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Regular organizado. |
| 19 | **Ellos han perdido las llaves en la calle esta noche** | They have lost the keys on the street tonight | `Ellos (Subject) + han perdido (Verb) + las llaves (Object) + en la calle (Place) + esta noche (Time)` | Regular perdido. |

### 📖 Lesson 28: Simple Future Tense (El Futuro Simple)

**Subtitle**: Intentions, Predictions, and Irregular Stems  
**Professor's Note**: *"Unlike present tense where endings vary by verb family, the simple future attaches endings directly to the full infinitive for -ar, -er, AND -ir verbs!"*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Master future endings (-é, -ás, -á, -emos, -éis, -án) on full infinitives
- Memorize the 12 irregular future stems (*tendr-, pondr-, saldr-, vendr-, podr-, sabr-, habr-, har-, dir-*)
- Express probability in the present (*¿Qué hora será? - Serán las tres*)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Regular Future Tense Endings

Attach these endings directly to the full infinitive of **-ar, -er, and -ir** verbs:

| Pronoun | Ending | Hablar (to speak) | Comer (to eat) | Vivir (to live) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | **-é** | hablaré | comeré | viviré |
| Tú | **-ás** | hablarás | comerás | vivirás |
| Él / Ella / Ud. | **-á** | hablará | comerá | vivirá |
| Nosotros | **-emos** | hablaremos | comeremos | viviremos |
| Ellos / Ellas / Uds. | **-án** | hablarán | comerán | vivirán |

> [!TIP]
> Notice that all endings except *nosotros (-emos)* carry a written accent mark!

##### Irregular Stems Grouping

Irregular verbs keep the standard endings, but alter their root stem:

1. **Drop Infinitive Vowel**: Caber -> *cabr-*, Haber -> *habr-*, Poder -> *podr-*, Saber -> *sabr-*, Querer -> *querr-*
2. **Drop Vowel & Add 'D'**: Poner -> *pondr-*, Salir -> *saldr-*, Tener -> *tendr-*, Venir -> *vendr-*, Valer -> *valdr-*
3. **Shortened Stems**: Hacer -> *har-*, Decir -> *dir-*

| Infinitive | Irregular Stem | Yo Conjugation | Nosotros Conjugation | Example Sentence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tener | **tendr-** | yo tendré | nosotros tendremos | Tendré tiempo mañana. |
| Poner | **pondr-** | yo pondré | nosotros pondremos | Pondré la mesa. |
| Salir | **saldr-** | yo saldré | nosotros saldremos | Saldremos a las seis. |
| Hacer | **har-** | yo haré | nosotros haremos | Haré la cena hoy. |
| Decir | **dir-** | yo diré | nosotros diremos | Diré la verdad. |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **mañana** | `mah-NYAH-nah` | tomorrow | Future time trigger |
| **el próximo año** | `ehl PROHK-see-moh AH-nyoh` | next year | Future time trigger |
| **tendré** | `tehn-DREH` | I will have | Irregular future of *tener* |
| **haré** | `ah-REH` | I will do/make | Irregular future of *hacer* |
| **diré** | `dee-REH` | I will say/tell | Irregular future of *decir* |
| **saldré** | `sahl-DREH` | I will exit/leave | Irregular future of *salir* |
| **podré** | `poh-DREH` | I will be able to | Irregular future of *poder* |
| **en el futuro** | `ehn ehl foo-TOO-roh` | in the future | General future trigger |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Yo viajaré a España en el avión mañana por la mañana.`  
   **English**: I will travel to Spain on the plane tomorrow morning.  
   *Breakdown*: Regular future tense of *viajar*.

2. **Spanish**: `Nosotros tendremos una reunión importante en la oficina el lunes.`  
   **English**: We will have an important meeting at the office on Monday.  
   *Breakdown*: Irregular stem *tendr-* for *tener*.

3. **Spanish**: `Ella hará la comida en la cocina esta noche.`  
   **English**: She will make the food in the kitchen tonight.  
   *Breakdown*: Irregular stem *har-* for *hacer*.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Mateo**: "¿Qué harás este fin de semana?" (*What will you do this weekend?*)
- **Lucía**: "Saldré con mis amigos al cine el sábado por la tarde." (*I will go out with my friends to the cinema on Saturday afternoon.*)
- **Mateo**: "¡Genial! Yo estudiaré para el examen final en mi casa." (*Great! I will study for the final exam at my home.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What is the correct future form for "We will have time"?  
**Options**: Tenemos tiempo, Teneremos tiempo, Tendremos tiempo, Habremos tiempo  
**Correct Answer**: `Tendremos tiempo`  
**Explanation**: Tener uses the irregular stem *tendr-*, taking the ending *-emos* for nosotros.

### 📖 Lesson 29: Conditional Tense (El Condicional Simple)

**Subtitle**: Hypothetical Scenarios, Polite Requests, and Future-in-the-Past  
**Professor's Note**: *"The conditional is your go-to tense for polite requests ('Me gustaría...') and hypothetical situations ('Viajaría si tuviera dinero')."*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Apply conditional endings (-ía, -ías, -ía, -íamos, -íais, -ían) to infinitives
- Use identical irregular stems as future tense (*tendría, pondría, habría, haría, diría*)
- Soften requests and express probability in the past (*Serían las cinco cuando llegó*)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### Regular Conditional Tense Endings

Attach these endings directly to the full infinitive of **-ar, -er, AND -ir** verbs:

| Pronoun | Ending | Viajar (to travel) | Comer (to eat) | Escribir (to write) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | **-ía** | viajaría | comería | escribiría |
| Tú | **-ías** | viajarías | comerías | escribirías |
| Él / Ella / Ud. | **-ía** | viajaría | comería | escribiría |
| Nosotros | **-íamos** | viajaríamos | comeríamos | escribiríamos |
| Ellos / Ellas / Uds. | **-ían** | viajarían | comerían | escribirían |

> [!NOTE]
> Every single form in the conditional tense carries a written accent mark over the **í**!

##### Irregular Conditional Stems (Same as Future Tense)

| Infinitive | Irregular Stem | Yo Conditional | Nosotros Conditional | Example Sentence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tener | **tendr-** | yo tendría | nosotros tendríamos | Yo tendría más paciencia. |
| Poner | **pondr-** | yo pondría | nosotros pondríamos | Pondríamos la mesa aquí. |
| Salir | **saldr-** | yo saldría | nosotros saldríamos | Saldríamos de paseo hoy. |
| Hacer | **har-** | yo haría | nosotros haríamos | ¿Qué harías tú en mi lugar? |
| Decir | **dir-** | yo diría | nosotros diríamos | Yo diría que sí. |
| Poder | **podr-** | yo podría | nosotros podríais | Podríamos ir al cine. |

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **me gustaría** | `meh goos-tah-REE-ah` | I would like | Polite request formula |
| **tendría** | `tehn-DREE-ah` | I would have | Irregular conditional of *tener* |
| **podría** | `poh-DREE-ah` | I could / would be able | Polite capability request |
| **haría** | `ah-REE-ah` | I would do/make | Irregular conditional of *hacer* |
| **sería** | `seh-REE-ah` | it would be | Conditional of *ser* |
| **viajaría** | `byah-hah-REE-ah` | I would travel | Conditional of *viajar* |
| **en tu lugar** | `ehn too loo-GAR` | in your place | Hypothetical advice trigger |
| **si pudiera** | `see poo-DYEH-rah` | if I could | Hypothetical trigger |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Me gustaría pedir un agua mineral en la mesa, por favor.`  
   **English**: I would like to order a mineral water at the table, please.  
   *Breakdown*: Softened polite request using *gustaría*.

2. **Spanish**: `Si tuviera dinero, yo compraría una casa en la playa este verano.`  
   **English**: If I had money, I would buy a house at the beach this summer.  
   *Breakdown*: Hypothetical clause with conditional *compraría*.

3. **Spanish**: `Nosotros haríamos el trabajo en la oficina si tuviéramos tiempo.`  
   **English**: We would do the work in the office if we had time.  
   *Breakdown*: Irregular stem *har-* in conditional.

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Camarero**: "¡Buenas tardes! ¿Qué desean tomar?" (*Good afternoon! What would you like to have?*)
- **Cliente**: "A mí me gustaría tomar un café con leche y una tostada, por favor." (*I would like to have a coffee with milk and a toast, please.*)
- **Camarero**: "¿Y para su acompañante?" (*And for your companion?*)
- **Cliente**: "Ella preferiría un té verde bien caliente." (*She would prefer a very hot green tea.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: How do you say "Could you help me, please?" politely in Spanish?  
**Options**: ¿Puedes ayudarme?, ¿Podrías ayudarme, por favor?, ¿Ayudarás a mí?, ¿Pudiste ayudarme?  
**Correct Answer**: `¿Podrías ayudarme, por favor?`  
**Explanation**: Podrías (conditional of poder) is the standard polite form for making requests.

### 📖 Lesson 30: Intro to Present Subjunctive (El Presente de Subjuntivo)

**Subtitle**: WEIRDOS Triggers, Yo-Form Roots, and Opposite Vowel Endings  
**Professor's Note**: *"The subjunctive mood expresses subjectivity, doubt, wishes, and recommendations rather than objective facts!"*

#### 🎯 Learning Objectives
- Understand WEIRDOS triggers (Wishes, Emotions, Impersonal expressions, Requests, Doubt, Ojalá, Speculation)
- Derive present subjunctive from 1st person singular present indicative (*yo*) root
- Apply opposite vowel endings (-ar verbs take -e endings; -er/-ir verbs take -a endings)

#### 📐 Grammar & Structural Rules

##### The 3-Step Formula for Present Subjunctive

To form the present subjunctive for regular and stem-changing verbs:

1. Start with the **yo** form of the present indicative: *hablo, como, escribo, tengo, hago*
2. Drop the **-o** ending: *habl-, com-, escrib-, teng-, hag-*
3. Add the **OPPOSITE** vowel endings!

##### Opposite Vowel Endings Table

| Pronoun | -AR Verbs (take -E endings) | -ER & -IR Verbs (take -A endings) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Yo | **-e** (hable) | **-a** (coma / viva) |
| Tú | **-es** (hables) | **-as** (comas / vivas) |
| Él / Ella / Ud. | **-e** (hable) | **-a** (coma / viva) |
| Nosotros | **-emos** (hablemos) | **-amos** (comamos / vivamos) |
| Ellos / Ellas / Uds. | **-en** (hablen) | **-an** (coman / vivan) |

##### The WEIRDOS Subjunctive Triggers

The subjunctive is required in dependent clauses after **que** when the main clause expresses:

- **W**ishes / Will: *Quiero que, Deseo que, Necesito que*
- **E**motions: *Me alegra que, Siento que, Me gusta que*
- **I**mpersonal Expressions: *Es importante que, Es necesario que, Es bueno que*
- **R**equests / Recommendations: *Recomiendo que, Pido que, Sugiero que*
- **D**oubt / Denial: *Dudo que, No creo que, No pienso que*
- **O**jalá: *¡Ojalá que...!* (God willing / I hope that)
- **S**peculation / Future Conjunctions: *Para que, Antes de que, En cuanto*

#### 🔤 Vocabulary Table

| Spanish Term | Phonetic Pronunciation | English Meaning | Usage Context |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **quiera** | `KYEH-rah` | s/he wants (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *querer* |
| **hable** | `AH-bleh` | s/he speaks (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *hablar* |
| **coma** | `KOH-mah` | s/he eats (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *comer* |
| **viva** | `BEE-bah` | s/he lives (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *vivir* |
| **tenga** | `TEHN-gah` | s/he has (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *tener* |
| **haga** | `AH-gah` | s/he does/makes (subjunctive) | Subjunctive of *hacer* |
| **dudo que** | `DOO-doh keh` | I doubt that | Doubt trigger |
| **ojalá** | `oh-hah-LAH` | hopefully / I wish | Universal hope trigger |

#### 💬 Core Example Sentences

1. **Spanish**: `Quiero que tú estudies español en la escuela cada día.`  
   **English**: I want you to study Spanish at school every day.  
   *Breakdown*: Wish trigger *Quiero que* + subjunctive *estudies*.

2. **Spanish**: `Es importante que nosotros comamos fruta en la cocina todas las mañanas.`  
   **English**: It is important that we eat fruit in the kitchen every morning.  
   *Breakdown*: Impersonal trigger *Es importante que* + subjunctive *comamos*.

3. **Spanish**: `Dudo que él tenga tiempo en la oficina hoy.`  
   **English**: I doubt that he has time at the office today.  
   *Breakdown*: Doubt trigger *Dudo que* + subjunctive *tenga* (from *tengo*).

#### 🗣️ Real Dialogue Context

- **Madre**: "Hijo, quiero que limpies tu habitación antes de salir." (*Son, I want you to clean your room before going out.*)
- **Hijo**: "Vale, mamá. Es necesario que termine la tarea primero." (*Okay, Mom. It is necessary that I finish homework first.*)
- **Madre**: "Ojalá hagas todo bien y rápido." (*Hopefully you do everything well and fast.*)

#### ⚡ Quick Practice Check

**Question**: What is the correct subjunctive form in: "Es necesario que tú _____ (comer) bien"?  
**Options**: comes, comas, coma, comamos  
**Correct Answer**: `comas`  
**Explanation**: Comer is an -er verb, so it takes opposite -a endings: yo coma, tú comas.

| 1 | **Quiero que tú hables español en la clase hoy** | I want you to speak Spanish in class today | `Quiero que (Subject) + tú hables (Verb) + español (Object) + en la clase (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Subjunctive opposite ending -es. |
| 2 | **Es necesario que nosotros comamos verduras en la cena esta noche** | It is necessary that we eat vegetables at dinner tonight | `Es necesario que (Subject) + nosotros comamos (Verb) + verduras (Object) + en la cena (Place) + esta noche (Time)` | Subjunctive opposite ending -amos. |
| 3 | **Dudo que él viva en Madrid en un piso este año** | I doubt that he lives in Madrid in an apartment this year | `Dudo que (Subject) + él viva (Verb) + en Madrid (Object) + en un piso (Place) + este año (Time)` | Subjunctive opposite ending -a. |
| 4 | **Recomiendo que ella estudie más en la biblioteca esta tarde** | I recommend that she study more in the library this afternoon | `Recomiendo que (Subject) + ella estudie (Verb) + más (Object) + en la biblioteca (Place) + esta tarde (Time)` | Subjunctive opposite ending -e. |
| 5 | **Ojalá que ellos tengan éxito en el examen mañana** | Hopefully they have success on the exam tomorrow | `Ojalá que (Subject) + ellos tengan (Verb) + éxito (Object) + en el examen (Place) + mañana (Time)` | Subjunctive teng- from yo tengo. |

| 13 | **Es preciso que nosotros traigamos los libros a la escuela el lunes** | It is necessary that we bring the books to school on Monday | `Es preciso que (Subject) + nosotros traigamos (Verb) + los libros (Object) + a la escuela (Place) + el lunes (Time)` | Subjunctive traig- from yo traigo. |

| 18 | **Recomiendo que ellos beban agua en el gimnasio hoy** | I recommend that they drink water in the gym today | `Recomiendo que (Subject) + ellos beban (Verb) + agua (Object) + en el gimnasio (Place) + hoy (Time)` | Subjunctive beban. |
